## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ वात्सप्रे᳓णो᳓पतिष्ठते एत᳓द्वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिर्विष्णुक्रमैः᳓ प्रजाः᳓ सृष्ट्वा᳓ [[ताभ्यो|ता᳓भ्यो]] वात्सप्रे᳓णायु᳓ष्यमकरोत्त᳓थैवै᳙त᳓द्य᳓जमानो विष्णुक्रमैः᳓ प्रजाः᳓ सृष्ट्वा᳓ [[ताभ्यो|ता᳓भ्यो]] वात्सप्रे᳓णायु᳓ष्यं करोति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He then stands by him worshipping with the Vātsapra chants (ascribed to Vātsaprī Bhalandana), because, Prajāpati by means of the Viṣṇu strides produced living beings and endowed them with longevity of life by means of the Vātsapra chants. In the like manner, the Yajamāna having produced the creatures by means of Viṣṇu strides, endows them with longevity of life by Vātsapra.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ वात्सप्रेणो᳘पतिष्ठते एतद्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिर्विष्णुक्रमैः᳘ प्रजाः᳘ सृष्ट्वा ताभ्यो वात्सप्रे᳘णायु᳘ष्यमकरोत्त᳘थै᳘वैतद्य᳘जमानो विष्णुक्रमैः᳘ प्रजाः᳘ सृष्ट्वा ताभ्यो वात्सप्रे᳘णायु᳘ष्यं करोति ॥ १ ॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/10 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ हैष᳓ दाक्षाय᳓णहस्तो य᳓द्वात्सप्रं᳓ त᳓स्माद्यं᳓ [[जातं|जातं᳓]] काम᳓येत ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/04_sa_haiSha_dAxAyaNahastaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Vātsapra is the hand (bestower) of longevity of life associated with life- breath.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स᳘ हैष᳘ दाक्षायणहस्तः[[!!]]॥  
(स्तो) य᳘द्वात्सप्रं त᳘स्माद्यं᳘ जातं᳘ काम᳘येत
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

स᳘ हैष᳘ दाक्षायणहस्तः᳟॥  
य᳘द्वात्सप्रं त᳘स्माद्यं᳘ जातं᳘ काम᳘येत
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

स हैष दाक्षायणहस्तः । यद्वात्सप्रम् । तस्माद् यं जातं कामयेत ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

प्रसङ्गात् कञ्चिदयज्ञसंयुक्तं प्रयोगं दर्शयति- **स हैष दाक्षायणहस्त** इति । 'दक्षः' प्राणः, तत्सम्बन्धि आयुष्यं 'दाक्षम्', तस्य 'अयनम्' प्रापकम्, तादृग्धस्तस्वरूपमिदं 'वात्सप्रम्' । तस्मात् उत्पन्नं शिशुम् आयुर्वृद्ध्यर्थं 'वात्सप्रेण अभिमृशेत्' ॥

वात्सप्रप्रसङ्गात् प्रागभिहितायाः “सुपर्णो ऽसि” इत्येतस्या विकृत्या यज्ञाद् बहिः प्रयोगं दर्शयति- **अथ यं कामयेत वीर्यवान्त्स्यादिति विकृत्यैनं पुरस्तादभिमन्त्रयेते**ति । 'पुरस्तात्' वात्सप्रेण अभिमर्शनात् पूर्वम् ॥ २ ॥
</details>
</details>
</div>

[[स᳓र्वमा᳓यु᳓रियादिति|स᳓र्वमा᳓यु᳓रियादि᳓ति]] वात्सप्रे᳓णैनमभि᳓मृशेत्त᳓दस्मै जाता᳓यायु᳓ष्यं करोति ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/05_sarvamAyuriyAditi.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

That is why in the case of a new-born, if one wishes “Let him live the full span of life”, he should be touched with chanting of Vātsapra and he thereby blesses him with longevity.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स᳘र्व्वमा᳘युरियादि᳘ति व्वात्सप्रे᳘णैनमभि᳘मृशेत्त᳘दस्मै जाता᳘यायु᳘ष्यं[[!!]] करोति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

स᳘र्वमा᳘युरियादि᳘ति वात्सप्रे᳘णैनमभि᳘मृशेत्त᳘दस्मै जाता᳘यायुष्यं᳘ करोति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

सर्वमायुरियादिति- वात्सप्रेणैनमभिमृशेत् । तदस्मै जातायायुष्यं करोति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

स᳘र्वमा᳘यु᳘रियादिति वात्सप्रे᳘णैनमभि᳘मृशेत्त᳘दस्मै जाता᳘यायु᳘ष्यं करोति
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

दिव्शब्दस्य विवक्षितमर्थमाह- **प्राणो वै दिव** इति । 'दिवः' दिव्प्रतिपाद्य इत्यर्थः । अथवा दिव इति पञ्चम्यन्तपदेन प्रातिपदिकमात्रं विवक्षित्वोक्तम्; दिव्प्रातिपदिकात् प्राणो विवक्षित इति ।

तत् कथमिति तत् समर्थयते- **प्राणादु वा एष प्रथममजायते**ति । प्राणात् उत्पत्तिः प्राक् काण्डादौ- "प्राणा वा ऋषयः"- इत्युपक्रम्य, "सप्त पुरुषानेकं पुरुषं करवामेति त एतान् सप्त पुरुषानेकं पुरुषमकुर्वन्"- (श. प. ६ । १ । १ । ३) इति प्रतिपादिता । सा चात्र 'वै'- शब्देन प्रसिद्धीक्रियते ॥
</details>
</details>
</div>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

"अस्मद् द्वितीयमिति"- अमुं द्वितीयं पादं व्याचष्टे- **यदेनमदो द्वितीयं पुरुषविधो ऽजनयदि**ति । 'यद्' यस्मात् 'एनम्' अग्निम् 'अदः' विप्रकृष्टे काले 'पुरुषविधः' यथा पुरुषः तदवयवी सम्पद्यते, तथा इष्टकाभिरजनयदित्यर्थः । अयमर्थो ऽपि तत्रैव प्राक् प्रतिपादितः- "स एव पुरुषः प्रजापतिरभवत् । स यः स पुरुषः प्रजापतिरभवत्, अयमेव स यो ऽयमग्निश्चीयते"- (श. प. ६ । १ । १ । ५) इति ।

तथाविधचयनसाध्याकारोत्पादनस्य अध्वर्युकर्तृकत्वात् ऋषिराह- **अस्मद् द्वितीयमि**ति ॥
</details>
</details>
</div>

“तृतीयमप्सु''- इत्येतद् व्याचष्टे- **यदेनमदस्तृतीयमद्भ्यो ऽजनयदि**ति । 'एनम्' आहुत्याधाररूपमित्यर्थः । अयमर्थो ऽपि तत्रैवोक्तः- "सो ऽपो ऽसृजत"- इत्युक्त्वा, "सो ऽकामयताभ्यो ऽद्भ्यो ऽधि प्रजायेयेति, सो ऽनया त्रय्या विद्यया सहापः प्राविशत् तत आण्डं समवर्त्तत"- इति, "अथ यो गर्भो ऽन्तरासीत् सो ऽग्निरसृज्यतेति च"- (श. प. ६ । १ । १ । ९-११ कं०) ॥
</details>
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/12_prajApatirvai_nRmaNA_agnirajasra.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

--'the

third time in the waters,'--inasmuch as he there did generate him a third time from the waters; --'he, the manly-minded, (kindling him) the imperishable,'--the manly-minded, doubtless, is Prajāpati; and the imperishable, Agni [^egg_540]; --'kindling him the mindful praises (jar) him,'--for he who kindles him generates him, mindful.

[^egg_540]: 284:1 The construction of the text here favoured by the author is very doubtful. It has probably to be construed,--'the third time (he, Agni, was born) in the waters, he, the manly-minded (or, friendly to men). Kindling him, the imperishable (Agni), the heedful (? or pious) one praises him,'--or perhaps, 'While kindling him, the considerate one praises him unceasingly.' A point which favours the author's construction is that, in verse 3, 'nr̥maṇaas' certainly refers not to Agni, but to him who generated him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“He who is gracious to men and unhurt”—Prajāpati is indeed gracious to human beings and Agni is unhurt.
</details>

</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

क उत्पादितवानिति, तदाह- **नृमणा** इति ।

नृमणः- शब्दस्य विवक्षितमर्थं दर्शयति- **प्रजापतिर्वै नृमणा** इति । नृविषयमनोयुक्तः इति तस्यार्थः ।

अजस्रशब्दं व्याचष्टे- **अग्निरजस्र** इति । 'अजस्रम्' अहिंसितम् अग्निमित्यर्थः ।
</details>
</details>

## ०४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

विद्मा᳓ ते अग्ने त्रेधा᳓ [[त्रयाणीत्यग्निर्वायु᳓रादित्य᳓|त्रया᳓णी᳓त्यग्नि᳓र्वायु᳓रादित्य᳓]] एता᳓नि हास्य᳓ ता᳓नि त्रेधा᳓ त्रया᳓णि विद्मा᳓ ते᳓ धा᳓म बि᳓भृता पुरुत्रे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“We know, O Agni, thy threefoldness in your three (forms)”—Agni, Vāyu and Āditya are his threefoldness in three (forms). “We know thy manifold abodes situated all around”—in as much as he (Agni) is here distributed many ways. “We know thy supreme name which is in secret”—for “The youngest”—that is that (supreme) name.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

विद्मा᳘ ते अग्ने त्रेधा᳘ त्रयाणीत्य᳘ग्नि᳘र्वायु᳘रादित्य᳘ एता᳘नि हास्य ता᳘नि त्रेधा᳘ त्रया᳘णि विद्मा ते धा᳘म बि᳘भृता पुरुत्रे᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[य᳓दिदं|य᳓दिदं᳓]] बहुधा᳓ विह्रिय᳓ते विद्मा᳓ ते᳓ ना᳓म परमं᳓ [[गुहा|गु᳓हा]] य᳓दि᳓ति य᳓विष्ठ इ᳓ति वा᳓ अस्य᳓ तं᳓ ना᳓म परमं᳓ गु᳓हा विद्मा᳓ [[त᳓मुत्सं|त᳓मु᳓त्सं]] य᳓त आजग᳓न्थे᳓त्या᳓पो वा᳓ उ᳓त्सोऽद्भ्यो᳓ वा᳓ एष᳓ [[प्रथम᳓माजगाम|प्रथम᳓मा᳓जगाम]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“We know that supreme secret abode whence thou art come”—the abode doubtless, is the (heavenly) waters, for from the waters he came first.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘दिदं बहुधा᳘ विह्रिय᳘ते विद्मा ते ना᳘म परमं᳘ गुहा यदि᳘ति य᳘विष्ठ इ᳘ति वा᳘ अस्य तं ना᳘म परमं गु᳘हा विद्मा तमुत्सं य᳘त आजगन्थेत्या᳘पो वा उ᳘त्सोऽद्भ्यो वा᳘ एष᳘ प्रथममाजगाम ॥ ४ ॥
</details>

## ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/13 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[समु᳓द्रेत्वा|समुद्रे᳓त्वा]] नृम᳓णा अप्स्व᳓न्तरि᳓ति प्रजा᳓पतिर्वै᳓ नृम᳓णा अप्सु᳓ त्वा प्रजा᳓पतिरि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“In the sea, the one gracious to human (produced) then in the waters”—the one gracious to humans is Prajāpati, thus in the waters Prajāpati (kindled) thee.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

समु᳘द्रेत्वा नृम᳘णा अप्स्व᳘न्तरि᳘ति प्रजा᳘पतिर्वै᳘ नृम᳘णा अप्सु᳘ त्वा प्रजा᳘पतिरि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

एत᳓न्नृच᳓क्षा [[ई᳓धे|ईधे]] [[दिवो᳙ऽग्न᳓|दिवो᳙ऽग्न]] [[ऊधन्नि᳓ति|ऊ᳓धन्नि᳓ति]] प्रजा᳓पतिर्वै᳓ नृच᳓क्षा आ᳓पो दिव᳓ ऊ᳓धस्तृती᳓ये त्वा र᳓जसि [[तस्थि᳓वांसमि᳓ति|तस्थिवां᳓समि᳓ति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“The one who watches the men kindled thee, O Agni! in the udder of the sky”—the one who watches the men, is doubtless, the Prajāpati and the udder of the sky is the waters. “Thee whilst standing in the third region”—the third region, doubtless, is the sky.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एत᳘न्नृच᳘क्षा ई᳘धे दिवो᳘ऽग्न᳘ ऊधन्नि᳘ति प्रजा᳘पतिर्वै᳘ नृच᳘क्षा आ᳘पो दिव ऊ᳘धस्तृती᳘ये त्वा र᳘जसि तस्थि᳘वाꣳसमि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

द्यौ᳓र्वै᳓ तृती᳓यं र᳓जोऽपामुप᳓स्थे महिषा᳓ अवर्धन्नि᳓ति ।

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/22_dyaurvai_tRtIyaM_rajo.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

--'the man-watcher hath kindled thee, O Agni, in the udder of the sky,'--the man-watcher, doubtless, is Prajāpati, and the udder of the sky is the waters ; --'thee, whilst standing in the third region,'--the third region, doubtless, is the sky; --'the buffaloes made (thee) grow in the lap of the waters ;' --the buffaloes, doubtless, are

the vital airs: thus, 'the vital airs made thee grow in the sky.'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

“The buffaloes made (thee) grow in the lap of the waters”—the buffaloes doubtless, are the vital airs. Thus “the vital airs made thee grow in the sky”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

द्यौर्व्वै᳘ तृती᳘यᳫँ᳭ र᳘जो ऽपामुप᳘स्थे महिषा᳘ ऽअवर्द्धन्नि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

मेतदु᳘पतिष्ठत इन्द्राग्नी वै स᳘र्वे
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

द्यौर्वै तृतीयं रजः । **"अपामुपस्थे महिषा अवर्द्धन्"**- (वा. सं. १२ । २०) इति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

द्यौर्वै᳘ तृती᳘यꣳ र᳘जोऽपामुप᳘स्थे महिषा᳘ अवर्द्धन्नि᳘ति
</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/35 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ता᳓ एता᳓ ए᳓कव्याख्याना एत᳓मेवा᳙भि᳓ ता᳓ [[आग्ने᳓यस्त्रिष्टु᳓भस्ता᳓|आग्नेय᳓स्त्रिष्टु᳓भस्ता᳓]] [[य᳓दाग्नेय्य᳙स्ते᳓नाग्नि᳓रथ|य᳓दाग्नेय्य᳙स्ते᳓नाग्नि᳓र᳓थ]] य᳓त्त्रिष्टु᳓भो य᳓दे᳓कादश [[तेनेन्द्र|ते᳓ने᳓न्द्र]] ऐन्द्रा᳓ग्नेऽग्नि᳓र्या᳓वानग्नि᳓र्या᳓वत्यस्य मा᳓त्रा ता᳓वतैवै᳙नमेत᳓दु᳓पतिष्ठते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

These (three chants) have the same explanation since they are related to Agni; they are Triṣṭubh chants regarding Agni and hence they are Agni (them- selves). Since they are in Triṣṭubh metre, with eleven (syllables), they are Indra. In Indra and Agni (one); as much as Agni is, so great is his size, with so much he stands in worship by him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ता᳘ एता ए᳘कव्याख्याना एत᳘मे᳘वाभि ता᳘ आग्ने᳘यस्त्रिष्टु᳘भस्ता य᳘दाग्ने᳘य्यस्ते᳘नाग्निरथ य᳘त्त्रिष्टु᳘भो यदे᳘कादश तेनेन्द्र ऐन्द्रा᳘ग्नेऽग्निर्या᳘वानग्निर्या᳘वत्यस्य मा᳘त्रा ता᳘वतै᳘वैनमेतदु᳘पतिष्ठते
</details>

</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indra and Agni are all the gods. And Agni is of all deities; as great as is his size, with so much, he stands worshipping by him.
</details>

</details>

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓द्वेव᳓ विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे᳓ भ᳓वतो विष्णुक्रमै᳓र्वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिरिमं᳓ लोक᳓म᳓सृजत वात्सप्रे᳓णाग्निं᳓ विष्णुक्रमै᳓र्वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिरन्त᳓रिक्षम᳓सृजत ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/27_yadveva_viShNukramavAtsapre_bhavataH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

7. And, again, why the Vishṇu-strides and the Vātsapra rite are (performed); --by the Vishṇu-strides Prajāpati created this world, and by the Vātsapra the fire (Agni); by the Vishṇu-strides Prajāpati created the air, and by the Vātsapra the wind (Vāyu);
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And again why the Viṣṇu strides and Vātsapra are performed—by the Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati created this world and by the Vātsapra, the Agni; by the Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati created the aerial region and by the Vātsapra, the Vāyu;
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

य᳘द्वेव᳘ व्विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भ᳘वतः॥  
(तो) व्विष्णुक्रमैर्व्वै᳘ प्रजापतिरिमं᳘ लोकम᳘सृजत व्वात्सप्रे᳘णाग्निं᳘ व्विष्णुक्रमैर्व्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिरन्त᳘रिक्षम᳘सृजत
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

य᳘द्वेव᳘ विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भ᳘वतः॥  
विष्णुक्रमैर्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिः स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘मभिप्रा᳘यात्स᳘ एत᳘दवसा᳘न
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

यद्वेव विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भवतः । विष्णुक्रमैर्वै प्रजापतिरिमं लोकमसृजत । वात्सप्रेणाग्निम् (१) । विष्णुक्रमैर्वै प्रजापतिरन्तरिक्षमसृजत ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

य᳘द्वेव᳘ विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भ᳘वतो विष्णुक्रमैर्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिरिमं᳘ लोकम᳘सृजत वात्सप्रे᳘णाग्निं᳘ विष्णुक्रमैर्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिरन्त᳘रिक्षम᳘सृजत

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

अथ पुनर्विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे समुच्चित्य [^१_२३५] (श. प. ६ । ७ । १ । १ ॥ ६ । ७ । ३ । १६ कं.) लोकतदधिष्ठातृदेवतादिसकलजगदुत्पादकतया स्तौति- **विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भवतो विष्णुक्रमैर्वै प्रजापतिरिमं लोकमसृजत वात्सप्रेणाग्निमि**त्यादिना । ‘वित्तं' धनम् । 'आशां' दिशम् । सर्वं निगदसिद्धम् ।

[^१_२३५]: स्रक्मप्रतिमोचनादि प्राग्वात्प्राद्विष्णुक्रमाः । का० श्रौ० सू० १६ । १७५ ।
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वात्सप्रे᳓ण रा᳓त्रिं विष्णुक्रमै᳓र्वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिः पूर्वपक्षा᳓न᳓सृजत वात्सप्रे᳓णापरपक्षा᳓न् ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

by the Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati created the bright fortnight and by the Vātsapra; the dark fortnight; by the Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati created the half-month and by the Vātsapra, the months; by the Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati created the seasons and by the Vātsapra, the year; thus the reason why he performs the Viṣṇu stride and Vātsapra is that he thereby even now creates everything.
</details>

## ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓द्वेव᳓ विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे᳓ भ᳓वतो विष्णुक्रमै᳓र्वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिः स्वर्गं᳓ लोक᳓मभि᳓प्रा᳓यात्स᳓ एत᳓दवसा᳓नमपश्यद्वात्सप्रं᳓ ते᳓ना᳓वास्यत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

And again why the Viṣṇustrides and Vātsapra—because it is only by Viṣṇu strides, Prajāpati went up to the heavens and saw the Vātsapra as the end of his journey (the means by which he unyoked the steeds) and by that he unyoked (the steeds) to avoid chafing;
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘द्वेव᳘ विष्णुक्रमवात्सप्रे भ᳘वतो विष्णुक्रमैर्वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिः स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘मभिप्रा᳘यात्स᳘ एत᳘दवसा᳘नमपश्यद्वात्सप्रं तेना᳘वास्यत्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓प्रदाहाय य᳓द्धि᳓ युक्तं᳓ न᳓ विमुच्य᳓ते प्र᳓ त᳓द्दह्यते ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

for if that which is yoked is not unloosed it is chafed.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

for, when the yoked animal is not unyoked, it is chafed.
</details>

<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

9. Having stridden the Vishṇu-strides, he then

forthwith stands by the fire worshipping it with the Vātsapra, just as one who has journeyed would forthwith unyoke. Men (proceed) after the manner of the gods: hence even now, when a troop of men have journeyed they forthwith unyoke.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having stridden the Viṣṇu strides, he then forthwith stands by the fire worshipping it with Vātsapra, just as one who has travelled, unyokes (the steeds) forthwith.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

</details>

अहोरात्रे᳘ वात्सप्र᳘महोते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/29 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓था प्रया᳓या᳓थ [[तदानीमेवा᳓पि|तदा᳓नीमेवा᳓पि]] विमुञ्चे᳓त्तादृ᳓क्त᳓द्देवा᳓नां वै᳓ विधा᳓म᳓नु मनुष्या᳙स्त᳓स्मादु हेद᳓मुत᳓ [[मानु᳓षो|मानुषो᳓]] ग्रा᳓मः प्रया᳓या᳓थ तदा᳓नीमेवा᳓वस्यति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Men (do) after the manner of the gods, hence even now, when a troop of men have journeyed, they immediately unyoke (the beasts that draw their vehicle).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓द्वा᳓ अहोरात्रे᳓ एव᳓ विष्णुक्रमा᳓ [[भ᳓वन्त्य᳓होरात्रे᳓|भ᳓वन्त्यहोरात्रे᳓]] वात्सप्र᳓महोरात्रे᳓ एव᳓ त᳓द्या᳓न्यहोरात्रे᳓ क्षेम्यो᳓ भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then, the Viṣṇu strides indeed are (performed) in a day and a night; and the Vātsapra (performed in another) day and night.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्वा᳘ अहोरात्रे᳘ एव᳘ विष्णुक्रमा भ᳘वन्त्य᳘होरात्रे᳘ वात्सप्र᳘महोरात्रे᳘ एव तद्या᳘न्यहोरात्रे᳘ क्षेम्यो᳘ भवति
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/43_tasmAdu_hedamuta_mAnuSho.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus in one day and one night, he travels, rests for a day and night and hence even now when a troop of men journey for a day and night, they take rest for a day and night.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

त᳘स्मादु हेद᳘मुत᳘ मानुषो ग्रा᳘मो ऽहोरात्रे᳘ या᳘त्वा ऽहोरात्रे᳘ क्षेम्यो᳘ भवति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

त᳘ᳫं᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘मभिप्रा᳘प्नुयादथ यद्भू᳘यो ऽर्धात्प᳘राङ् हैत᳘ᳫं᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘मतिप्र᳘णश्येद᳘थ य᳘दर्धं क्र᳘मते ऽर्ध᳘मुपति᳘ष्ठते त᳘त्सम्प्रति᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘माप्त्वा वि᳘मुञ्चते॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

तस्मादु हेदमुत मानुषो ग्रामो ऽहोरात्रे यात्वा, अहोरात्रे क्षेम्यो भवति ॥ १० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

त᳘स्मादु हेद᳘मुत मानु᳘षो ग्रा᳘मोऽहोरात्रे᳘ या᳘त्वाहोरात्रे᳘ क्षेम्यो᳘ भवति ॥ १० ॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/10 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

म᳓ध्ये [[ह᳓|ह]] संवत्सर᳓स्य स्वर्गो᳓ [[लोकः|लोकः᳓]] स᳓ य᳓त्क᳓नीयोऽर्धा᳓त्क्र᳓मेत ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Only for one half of the year, he strides the Viṣṇu strides and for one half he worships the fire with Vātsapra, for the world of heaven falls in the middle of the year. Thus were he to stride for less than half (year), he would not reach that world of heaven; were he to do so for more than half year, he would have passed beyond that world of heaven and missed it, but when he strides for one half and worships the fire for one half, he unyokes forthwith after reaching the world of heaven.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

म᳘ध्ये ह᳘ संवत्सर᳘स्य स्वर्गो᳘ लोकः᳘ स यत्क᳘नीयोऽर्धात्क्र᳘मेत
</details>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्भू᳓योऽर्धा᳓प᳓राङ् हैतं᳓ स्वर्गं᳓ [[लोकमभिप्र᳓णश्येद᳓थ|लोक᳓मभिप्र᳓णश्येद᳓थ]] य᳓दर्धं᳓ [[क्रमतेऽर्धमुपति᳓ष्ठते|क्र᳓मतेऽर्ध᳓मुपति᳓ष्ठते]] त᳓त्संप्रति᳓ स्वर्गं᳓ लोक᳓माप्त्वा᳓ [[विमुच्यते|वि᳓मुच्यते]] ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/46_atha_yadbhUyo.ardhAtparA~NhaitaM.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

अ᳘थ यद्भू᳘यो ऽर्द्धात्प᳘राङ्हैत᳘ᳫँ᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘मतिप्र᳘णश्येद᳘थ य᳘दर्द्धं क्र᳘मते ऽर्द्ध᳘मुपति᳘ष्ठते त᳘त्सम्प्रति᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘माप्त्वा व्वि᳘मुञ्चते॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

आ᳘ इदᳫं स᳘र्वम् प्रजा᳘पतिः प्रजनयिष्यं᳘श्च प्रजनयित्वा᳘ चाहोरात्रा᳘भ्यामुभय᳘तः प᳘रिगृह्णाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यद्भूयो ऽर्द्धात्- पराङ् हैतं स्वर्गं लोकमतिप्रणश्येत् । अथ यदर्द्धं क्रमते- अर्द्धमुपतिष्ठते- तत् सम्प्रति स्वर्गं लोकमाप्त्वा विमुञ्चते ॥ ११ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳘थ यद्भू᳘योऽर्धाप᳘राङ् हैत᳘ꣳ᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोकमभिप्र᳘णश्येद᳘थ य᳘दर्धं क्रमतेऽर्धमुपति᳘ष्ठते त᳘त्संप्रति᳘ स्वर्गं᳘ लोक᳘माप्त्वा विमुच्यते ॥ ११ ॥
</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/4 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ता᳓भ्यां वै᳓ [[विपर्यासमेति|विपर्या᳓समेति]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He proceeds with these two alternatively, even as one would travel a long distance, unyoking (intermittently).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ता᳘भ्यां वै᳘ विपर्यासमेति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/4 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (5/22 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓था महा᳓न्तम᳓ध्वानं विमो᳓कं [[सम᳓श्नुवीत᳓|समश्नुवीत᳓]] [[ता᳓दृक्तस्य|तादृ᳓क्त᳓स्य]] [[वै|वै᳓]] पुर᳓स्ताच्चोप᳓रिष्टाच्चोभे᳓ विष्णुक्रम᳓वात्सप्रे [[समस्यत्य᳓हर्वै᳓|स᳓मस्यत्य᳓हर्वै᳓]] विष्णुक्रमा᳓ रा᳓त्रिर्वात्सप्र᳓म् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

In the beginning (before the dīkṣā) and at the end (after the dīkṣā), he simultaneously performs Viṣṇu stride and Vātsapra, for the Viṣṇu strides are (in) the days the Vātsapra (in) the nights;
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘था महा᳘न्तम᳘ध्वानं विमो᳘कꣳ सम᳘श्नुवीत᳘ ता᳘दृक्तस्य वै पुर᳘स्ताच्चोप᳘रिष्टाच्चोभे᳘ विष्णुक्रम᳘वात्सप्रे समस्यत्य᳘हर्वै᳘ विष्णुक्रमा रा᳘त्रिर्वात्सप्र᳘म्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/28 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[एत᳓द्वा|एत᳓द्वा᳓]] इदं᳓ स᳓र्वं प्रजा᳓पतिः प्रजनयिष्यं᳓श्च प्रजनयित्वा᳓ चाहोरात्रा᳓भ्यामुभय᳓तः प᳓र्यगृह्णात्त᳓थैवै᳙त᳓द्य᳓जमान इदं᳓ स᳓र्वं प्रजनयिष्यं᳓श्च प्रजनयित्वा᳓ चाहोरात्रा᳓भ्यामुभय᳓तो विमोकं᳓ प᳓रिगृह्णाति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

and Prajāpati, both before and after creation covered it on both sides with day and night. In the like manner, the Yajamāna now, when he is about to generate and when he has generated this universe, encloses it on both sides by day and night (simultane- ously).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एतद्वा इदꣳ स᳘र्वं प्रजा᳘पतिः प्रजनयिष्य᳘ꣳ᳘श्च प्रजनयित्वा᳘ चाहोरात्रा᳘भ्यामुभय᳘तः प᳘र्यगृह्णात्त᳘थै᳘वैतद्य᳘जमान इदꣳ स᳘र्वं प्रजनयिष्य᳘ꣳ᳘श्च प्रजनयित्वा᳘ चाहोरात्रा᳘भ्यामुभय᳘तो विमोकं᳘ प᳘रिगृह्णाति ॥ १२ ॥
</details>

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (5/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[त᳓दा᳓हुः|त᳓दाहुः]] य᳓द᳓हर्विष्णुक्रमा᳓ रा᳓त्रिर्वात्सप्र᳓म᳓थोभे᳓ एवा᳓ह᳓न्भ᳓वतो न᳓ [[रात्र्यां|रा᳓त्र्यां]] कथ᳓मस्या᳓पि [[रात्र्यां|रा᳓त्र्यां]] [[कृते|कृते᳓]] भवत᳓ [[इति|इ᳓ति]] ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/49_tadAhuH_yadaharviShNukramA.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

13. As to this they say, 'If the Vishṇu-strides are the day, and the Vātsapra the night, and both of them are (performed) during the day, not during the night, how then are they both performed for (or by) him also during the night?'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

As to this they say 'If the Viṣṇu strides are the day (meant to be performed in the day) and the Vātsapra, the nights (meant to be performed in the nights) and both are done in the night, (simultaneously), how come they also become done in the night?
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

त᳘दाहुः॥  
(र्य) यद᳘हर्व्विष्णुक्रमा रा᳘त्रिर्व्वात्सप्रम᳘थोभे᳘ ऽएवा᳘हन्भ᳘वतो न रा᳘त्र्यां कथ᳘मस्या᳘पि रा᳘त्र्यां कृते᳘ भवत ऽइ᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

स यद᳘हः संनिवप्स्यन्त्स्या᳘त् तद᳘हः प्रातरु᳘दित आदित्ये भ᳘स्मैव᳘ प्रथममु᳘द्वपति भ᳘स्मोदु᳘प्य वा᳘चं वि᳘सृजते वा᳘चं विसृ᳘ज्य स
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

तदाहुः- यदहर्विष्णुक्रमाः, रात्रिर्वात्सप्रम्- अथोभे ऽएवाहन्भवतो, न रात्र्याम् । कथमस्यापि रात्र्यां कृते भवत इति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

तदा᳘हुः यद᳘हर्विष्णुक्रमा रा᳘त्रिर्वात्सप्रम᳘थोभे᳘ एवाहन्भ᳘वतो न रात्र्यां कथ᳘मस्या᳘पि रात्र्यां कृते भवत इति
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

"अहर्वै विष्णुक्रमो रात्रिर्वात्सप्रम्" इत्यत्र काचिच्छङ्का समुन्मिषति, तामनूद्य समाधत्ते- **तदाहुर्यदहरि**ति । 'उभे एव' 'अहन्' अहनि निर्वर्त्ये 'भवतः,' 'न रात्र्याम्;' 'कथम्' 'अस्य' यजमानस्य 'रात्र्याम्' अपि 'कृते भवत' ।
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

Well, on that (first) occasion, when he is being initiated, he, at the outset, combines both (performances) in the afternoon; for the afternoon is the same thing as the night.

Well, on that eve of his being initiated, he at the outset combines both (the performances) in the afternoon; for the afternoon is (as good as) night.
</details>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/31 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

रा᳓त्रिर्हि᳓ त᳓द्य᳓दपराह्णो᳓ऽथैने [[एतत्सन्निवप्स्य᳓न्नुप᳓रिष्टात्पूर्वाह्ण᳓|एत᳓त्सन्निवप्स्य᳓न्नुप᳓रिष्टात्पूर्वाह्ण᳓]] [[उ᳓भे|उभे᳓]] [[स᳓मस्यत्य᳓हर्हैत᳓द्या᳓पूर्वाह्न|स᳓मस्यत्य᳓हर्हैत᳓द्या᳓पूर्वा᳓ह्न]] एव᳓मु हास्यो᳓भे᳓ एवा᳓हन्कृते᳓ भ᳓वत उभे᳓ रा᳓त्र्याम् ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/06/07/4/51_rAtrirhaitadyadaparAhNo.athaine.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

Then throwing them thus together, he at the end combines them both in the forenoon; for the forenoon is the same thing as the day; and in this way they are both performed during the day, and both during the night.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Thus having combined them together, later (after the creation), he continues both in the forenoon, for the forenoon is (as good as) the day. In this way both happen to be performed in the day and both in the night.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

रा᳘त्रिर्हैतद्य᳘दपराह्णो᳘ ऽथैने ऽएत᳘त्सन्निवप्स्य᳘न्नुप᳘रिष्टात्पूर्वाह्ण᳘ ऽउभे स᳘मस्यत्य᳘हर्हैतद्य᳘त्पूर्वाह्ण᳘ ऽएव᳘मु हास्योभे᳘ ऽएवा᳘हन् कृते भ᳘वत ऽउभे रा᳘त्र्याम्॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

य᳘थैव त᳘स्याभ्यवह᳘रणं त᳘थापादा᳘य भ᳘स्मनः प्रत्ये᳘त्योखा᳘यामोप्यो᳘पतिष्ठते᳘ ऽथ प्रा᳘यश्चित्ती करोति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

रात्रिर्हैतद्- यदपराह्णः । अथैने ऽएतत् सन्निवप्स्यन्नुपरिष्टात् पूर्वाह्ण ऽउभे समस्यति । अहर्हैतद्- यत् पूर्वाह्णः । एवमु हास्योभे ऽएवाहन् कृते भवतः- उभे रात्र्याम् ॥ १३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

रा᳘त्रिर्हि तद्य᳘दपराह्णो᳘ऽथैने एतत्सन्निवप्स्य᳘न्नुप᳘रिष्टात्पूर्वाह्ण᳘ उ᳘भे स᳘मस्यत्य᳘हर्हैतद्या᳘पूर्वाह्न एव᳘मु हास्योभे᳘ एवा᳘हन्कृते भ᳘वत उभे रा᳘त्र्याम् ॥ १३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

अस्तु, रात्रेः कथं प्राप्तिरिति, तत्राह- **रात्रिर्हैतद् यदपराह्ण** इति ।

इत्थमादौ समासकारणमुक्त्वा अन्ते ऽपि दर्शयति- **अथैने एतत् सन्निवप्स्यन्नि**ति । 'उपरिष्टात्' संवत्सरावसाने । शिष्टं पूर्ववत् । 'एवम्' आद्यन्तयोः समस्यानुष्ठानेन 'उभे' अपि सर्वस्मिन्नहनि समस्य कृते भवतः । तथा सर्वस्यामपि 'रात्र्याम्' 'उभे' अपि समस्य 'कृते भवतः' ॥ १३ ॥
</details>
</details>
</div>

वा᳓चं [[विसृज्य|विसृ᳓ज्य]] [[समि᳓धमा᳓द᳓धाति|समि᳓धमा᳓दधाति]] [[समि᳓धमा᳓धाय|समि᳓धमाधा᳓य]] भ᳓स्मापो᳙ऽभ्य᳓वहरति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Having removed the ashes, he releases his speech. Having released his speech, he offers a samidhā. After putting on a kindling stick, he throws the ashes into the water.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

वा᳘चं विसृज्य समि᳘धमा᳘द᳘धाति समि᳘धमा᳘धाय भ᳘स्मा᳘पोऽभ्य᳘वहरति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/13 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓थैव᳓ [[यजुस्त᳓स्याभ्यवह᳓रणं|य᳓जुस्त᳓स्याभ्यवह᳓रणं]] त᳓थापादा᳓य भ᳓स्मनः प्रत्ये᳓त्योखा᳓यामो᳓प्यो᳓पतिष्ठते ॥

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Just as he throws the ashes, he collects some of the ashes and returns and puts them into the ukhā and stands by it worshipfully.
</details>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ प्रा᳓यश्चित्तीकरोति स᳓ य᳓दि विष्णुक्रमी᳓यम᳓हः स्या᳓द्विष्णुक्रमा᳓न्क्रान्त्वा᳓ वात्सप्रे᳓णो᳓पतिष्ठेता᳓थ य᳓दि वात्सप्री᳓यं [[वात्सप्रेणो᳓पस्था᳓य|वात्सप्रे᳓णोपस्था᳓य]] [[विष्णुक्रमा᳓न्क्रान्त्वा|विष्णुक्रमा᳓न्क्रान्त्वा᳓]] वात्सप्र᳓मन्ततः᳓ कुर्या᳓त् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If that day happens to be the one when Viṣṇu striding is due (to be performed) he does the Viṣṇu striding and then stands by and performs Vātsapra. If that day happens to be the one when the Vātsapra is due, he stands by worshipping with Vātsapra and then performs the Viṣṇu strides and finally performs the Vātsapra.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ प्रा᳘यश्चित्तीकरोति स य᳘दि विष्णुक्रमी᳘यम᳘हः स्या᳘द्विष्णुक्रमा᳘न्क्रान्त्वा᳘ वात्सप्रेणो᳘पतिष्ठेता᳘थ य᳘दि वात्सप्री᳘यं वात्सप्रेणो᳘पस्था᳘य विष्णुक्रमा᳘न्क्रान्त्वा वात्सप्र᳘मन्ततः᳘ कुर्यात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/16 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

न᳓ विष्णुक्रमा᳓नन्ततः᳓ कुर्याद्य᳓था प्रया᳓य [[न|न᳓]] विमुञ्चे᳓त्तादृ᳓क्त᳓द᳓थ य᳓द्वात्सप्र᳓मन्ततः᳓ करो᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Let him not conclude by performing Viṣṇu strides, for that would amount to having gone for a drive, failing to unyoke (the steed).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[प्र᳓तिष्ठा|प्रतिष्ठा᳓]] वै᳓ वात्सप्रं᳓ य᳓था प्रतिष्ठाप᳓येदवसाय᳓येत्तादृ᳓क्त᳓स्मादु वात्सप्र᳓मेवा᳙न्ततः᳓ कुर्यात् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he concludes with Vātsapra which is a (legitimate) halt, it would amount to unyoking after reaching the halting place. Let him therefore conclude by performing Vātsapra. (Fourth Brāhmaṇa Ends) (Chapter Seven Ends)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

प्र᳘तिष्ठा वै᳘ वात्सप्रं य᳘था प्रतिष्ठाप᳘येदवसाय᳘येत्तादृक्त᳘स्मादु वात्सप्र᳘मे᳘वान्ततः᳘ कुर्यात् ॥ १५ ॥
</details>
